-- ******************************************************************** --
-- author: guzhengfeng
-- create time: 2023/07/07 11:24:09 GMT+08:00
-- desc：供应商往来重分类后自然账龄 DWRFIN.DWR_AP_SUP_TRANS_RCSF_AGING_F
-- update：2023/07/11 guzhengfeng 报表下线总账科目、特别总账字段
-- update：2023/07/12 guzhengfeng 1、修改各科目分类账龄逻辑；2、增加限制条件，过账日期<=查询日期，清账日期>查询日期 或 清账日期 = '00000000'
-- ******************************************************************** --
WITH AGING AS (
SELECT 
    T.FISAL_YEAR                                -- 财年
    ,T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER                   -- 特别总账
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,T.VOUCHER_TYPE                             -- 凭证类型
    ,T.VOUCHER_CODE                             -- 凭证编码
    ,T.VOUCHER_LINE_CODE                        -- 凭证行编码
    ,T.VOUCHER_DATE                             -- 凭证日期
    ,T.POTING_DATE                              -- 过账日期
    ,replace(T.DUE_DATE,'-','') DUE_DATE        -- 到期日
    ,T.CLEARED_DATE                             -- 清账日期
    ,T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                -- 原币期末余额
    ,T.VOUCHER_CURRENCY                         -- 凭证货币
    ,T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                 -- 本币期末余额
    ,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY                           -- 本币货币
    ,T.ACTUAL_EXCHANGE_RATE                     -- 实际汇率
    ,CASE WHEN T.FISAL_YEAR < 2019 THEN T.VOUCHER_DATE
        ELSE T.POTING_DATE
    END DATE1                                   -- 用于计算自然账龄
FROM 
    DWRFIN.DWR_AP_SUPPLIER_TRANSACTION_D T
WHERE SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,4) IN ('预提费用','预付账款','应付账款','其他应付','其他应收','应付票据')
UNION ALL
SELECT 
    T1.FISAL_YEAR                               -- 财年
    ,T1.COMP_CODE                               -- 公司代码
    ,T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                  -- 总账科目
    ,T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC             -- 总账科目描述
    ,T1.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER                  -- 特别总账
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                 -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                 -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_CODE                           -- 供应商代码
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_NAME                           -- 供应商全称
    ,T1.PAY_TERM_CODE                           -- 付款条款
    ,T1.PAY_TERM_DESC                           -- 付款条款描述
    ,T1.VOUCHER_TYPE                            -- 凭证类型
    ,T1.VOUCHER_CODE                            -- 凭证编码
    ,T1.VOUCHER_LINE_CODE                       -- 凭证行编码
    ,T1.VOUCHER_DATE                            -- 凭证日期
    ,T1.POTING_DATE                             -- 过账日期
    ,T1.DUE_DATE                                -- 到期日
    ,T1.CLEARED_DATE                            -- 清账日期
    ,T1.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT               -- 原币期末余额
    ,T1.VOUCHER_CURRENCY                        -- 凭证货币
    ,T1.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                -- 本币期末余额
    ,T1.LOCAL_CURRENCY                          -- 本币货币
    ,T1.ACTUAL_EXCHANGE_RATE                    -- 实际汇率
    ,CASE WHEN T1.FISAL_YEAR < 2019 THEN T1.VOUCHER_DATE
        ELSE T1.POTING_DATE
    END DATE1                                   -- 用于计算自然账龄    
FROM 
    DWRFIN.DWR_AP_SUPPLIER_TRANSACTION_EST_D T1
)
/*
将限定条件数据插入中间表 DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M2_D
*/
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M2_D
SELECT 
    T.FISAL_YEAR                                -- 财年
    ,T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SPECIAL_GENERAL_LEDGER                   -- 特别总账
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,T.VOUCHER_TYPE                             -- 凭证类型
    ,T.VOUCHER_CODE                             -- 凭证编码
    ,T.VOUCHER_LINE_CODE                        -- 凭证行编码
    ,T.VOUCHER_DATE                             -- 凭证日期
    ,T.POTING_DATE                              -- 过账日期
    ,T.DUE_DATE                                 -- 到期日
    ,T.CLEARED_DATE                             -- 清账日期
    ,T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                -- 原币期末余额
    ,T.VOUCHER_CURRENCY                         -- 凭证货币
    ,T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                 -- 本币期末余额
    ,T.LOCAL_CURRENCY                           -- 本币货币
    ,T.ACTUAL_EXCHANGE_RATE                     -- 实际汇率
    ,T.DATE1                                    -- 用于计算自然账龄
    ,day( to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp('${PERIOD_ID_D}','yyyyMMdd'))) - to_date(from_unixtime(unix_timestamp(T.DATE1,'yyyyMMdd'))) ) AS DUE_DAYS            -- 自然到期天数
    ,CASE WHEN SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,4) = '预提费用' THEN 'A'
          WHEN SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,4) = '预付账款' THEN 'B'
          WHEN SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,4) = '其他应付' THEN 'D'
          WHEN SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,4) = '其他应收' THEN 'E'
          WHEN SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,7) = '应付票据-银承' THEN 'F'
          WHEN SUBSTR(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,1,7) = '应付票据-商业' THEN 'G'
        ELSE 'C'
    END ACCOUNT_TYPE                            -- 科目分类
FROM 
    AGING T
WHERE T.POTING_DATE <= '${PERIOD_ID_D}'
AND (T.CLEARED_DATE = '00000000' OR T.CLEARED_DATE > '${PERIOD_ID_D}')
;

/* 补充每个供应商的科目分类，插入中间表3 */
WITH AP_AGING AS
(
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,T.DUE_DAYS                                 -- 自然到期天数
    ,M.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
FROM 
(
SELECT ACCOUNT_TYPE FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M2_D GROUP BY ACCOUNT_TYPE
) M
LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,T.DUE_DAYS                                 -- 自然到期天数
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
FROM 
    DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M2_D T
) T
ON 1 = 1
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,T.DUE_DAYS                                 -- 自然到期天数
    ,M.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
)
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'A' THEN '预提' 
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN '预付' 
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN '应付' 
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'D' THEN '其他应付'
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'E' THEN '其他应收'
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'F' THEN '应付票据银票'
        ELSE '应付票据商票'
    END GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC             -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,NVL(T1.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT,0)        -- 原币期末余额
    ,NVL(T1.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT,0)         -- 本币期末余额
    ,T.DUE_DAYS                                 -- 自然到期天数
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
FROM 
    AP_AGING T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT                   -- 总账科目
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                -- 原币期末余额
    ,T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT                 -- 本币期末余额
    ,T.DUE_DAYS                                 -- 自然到期天数
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
FROM 
    DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M2_D T
) T1
ON  NVL(T.COMP_CODE,'NULL') = NVL(T1.COMP_CODE,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT,'NULL') = NVL(T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,'NULL') = NVL(T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE,'NULL') = NVL(T1.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC,'NULL') = NVL(T1.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.SUPPLIER_CODE,'NULL') = NVL(T1.SUPPLIER_CODE,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.SUPPLIER_NAME,'NULL') = NVL(T1.SUPPLIER_NAME,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.PAY_TERM_CODE,'NULL') = NVL(T1.PAY_TERM_CODE,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.PAY_TERM_DESC,'NULL') = NVL(T1.PAY_TERM_DESC,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.DUE_DAYS,'NULL') = NVL(T1.DUE_DAYS,'NULL')
AND NVL(T.ACCOUNT_TYPE,'NULL') = NVL(T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE,'NULL')

;

/* 计算重分类前原币期末余额，插入中间表4 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M4_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','C','D','F','G') THEN -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_O,0)
            WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('B') THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_1,0) <> 0 THEN T1.SUM_1 WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_2,0) <> 0 AND T1.SUM_1 = '' THEN 0 ELSE 0 END
        ELSE NVL(T1.SUM_O,0) 
    END AS ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING          -- 重分类前原币期末余额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O  -- 重分类前原币期末余额
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) 
        ELSE 0
    END AS SUM_1                                -- 科目分类为：B时，为合计1
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT)
        ELSE 0
    END AS SUM_2                                -- 科目分类为：C，为合计2
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1    
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','C','D','F','G') THEN -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_O,0)
            WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('B') THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_1,0) <> 0 THEN T1.SUM_1 WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_2,0) <> 0 AND T1.SUM_1 = '' THEN 0 ELSE 0 END
        ELSE NVL(T1.SUM_O,0) 
    END
;

/* 计算重分类前本币期末余额，插入中间表5 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M5_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','C','D','F','G') THEN -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_O,0)
            WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('B') THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_1,0) <> 0 THEN T1.SUM_1 WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_2,0) <> 0 AND T1.SUM_1 = '' THEN 0 ELSE 0 END
        ELSE NVL(T1.SUM_O,0) 
    END AS LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING           -- 重分类前本币期末余额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O  -- 重分类前本币期末余额
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) 
        ELSE 0
    END AS SUM_1                                -- 科目分类为：B时，为合计1
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT)
        ELSE 0
    END AS SUM_2                                -- 科目分类为：C，为合计2
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1    
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','C','D','F','G') THEN -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_O,0)
            WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('B') THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_1,0) <> 0 THEN T1.SUM_1 WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_2,0) <> 0 AND T1.SUM_1 = '' THEN 0 ELSE 0 END
        ELSE NVL(T1.SUM_O,0) 
    END
;

/* 计算重分类后原币期末余额，插入中间表6 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M6_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'A' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 THEN 0 ELSE -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) ) 
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'D' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) ELSE -1 * ( NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'E' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) ELSE NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) END
        ELSE -1 * ( T6.SUM_3 )
    END AS ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF       -- 重分类后原币期末余额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_AB -- 重分类后原币期末余额（科目分类为：A和B的合计）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C  -- 重分类后原币期末余额（科目分类为：C）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_1  -- 重分类后原币期末余额（总账科目为：0021810117、0011330100）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_2  -- 重分类后原币期末余额（总账科目为：0021810140、0021810300、0021810200）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810140','0021810300','0021810200')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_22 -- 重分类后原币期末余额（总账科目为：0011330140、0011330120）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_3  -- 重分类后原币期末余额（科目分类为：应付票据银票或者应付票据商票）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F','G')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'A' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 THEN 0 ELSE -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) ) 
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'D' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) ELSE -1 * ( NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'E' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) ELSE NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) END
        ELSE -1 * ( T6.SUM_3 )
    END                                        -- 重分类后原币期末余额
;

/* 计算重分类后本币期末余额，插入中间表7 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M7_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'A' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 THEN 0 ELSE -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) ) 
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'D' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) ELSE -1 * ( NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'E' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) ELSE NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) END
        ELSE -1 * ( T6.SUM_3 )
    END AS LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF       -- 重分类后本币期末余额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_AB  -- 重分类后本币期末余额（科目分类为：A和B的合计）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C   -- 重分类后本币期末余额（科目分类为：C）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_1   -- 重分类后本币期末余额（总账科目为：0021810117、0011330100）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_2  -- 重分类后本币期末余额（总账科目为：0021810140、0021810300、0021810200）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810140','0021810300','0021810200')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_22 -- 重分类后本币期末余额（总账科目为：0011330140、0011330120）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_3  -- 重分类后本币期末余额（科目分类为：应付票据银票或者应付票据商票）
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F','G')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'A' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 THEN 0 ELSE -1 * NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'B' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'C' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) ) 
                                              WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T2.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T2.SUM_C,0)
                                            ELSE 0 END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'D' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) ELSE -1 * ( NVL(T4.SUM_2,0) ) END
          WHEN T.ACCOUNT_TYPE = 'E' THEN CASE WHEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0)  > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_1,0) + NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) ELSE NVL(T5.SUM_22,0) END
        ELSE -1 * ( T6.SUM_3 )
    END
;

/* 计算科目分类为预提的账龄，插入中间表8 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M8_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,'A' AS ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,( T.AGING_0_30 + T.AGING_31_60 + T.AGING_61_90 + T.AGING_91_180 + T.AGING_181_365 ) AS AGING_1_YEAR                                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0 
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS
        ELSE 0
    END AGING_1826                                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码    
    ,T.AGING_0_30
    ,T.AGING_31_60
    ,T.AGING_61_90
    ,T.AGING_91_180
    ,T.AGING_181_365
    ,T.AGING_366_730
    ,T.AGING_731_1095
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825
    ,T.AGING_1826
    ,(CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END) AGING_MINUS
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,NVL(T1.AGING_0_30,0)           AS AGING_0_30               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T2.AGING_31_60,0)          AS AGING_31_60              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T3.AGING_61_90,0)          AS AGING_61_90              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T4.AGING_91_180,0)         AS AGING_91_180             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T5.AGING_181_365,0)        AS AGING_181_365            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T6.AGING_366_730,0)        AS AGING_366_730            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T7.AGING_731_1095,0)       AS AGING_731_1095           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T8.AGING_1096_1460,0)      AS AGING_1096_1460          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T9.AGING_1461_1825,0)      AS AGING_1461_1825          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T10.AGING_1826,0)          AS AGING_1826               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_0_30         -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_31_60        -- 31-60日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_61_90        -- 61-90日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_91_180       -- 91-180日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 91 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 180
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_181_365      -- 181-365日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_366_730      -- 1-2年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 366 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 730
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_731_1095     -- 2-3年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 731 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1095
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T7
ON T.COMP_CODE = T7.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T7.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1096_1460    -- 3-4年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1096 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T8
ON T.COMP_CODE = T8.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T8.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1461_1825    -- 4-5年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T9
ON T.COMP_CODE = T9.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T9.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1826         -- 5年以上账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                           -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T10
ON T.COMP_CODE = T10.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T10.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T1.AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T2.AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T3.AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T4.AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T5.AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T7.AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T8.AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T9.AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T10.AGING_1826                                             -- 5年以上账龄段金额
) T
) T
) T
;

/* 计算科目分类为预付的账龄，插入中间表9 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M9_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,'B' AS ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,( T.AGING_0_30 + T.AGING_31_60 + T.AGING_61_90 + T.AGING_91_180 + T.AGING_181_365 ) AS AGING_1_YEAR                                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0 
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS
        ELSE 0
    END AGING_1826                                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码    
    ,T.AGING_0_30
    ,T.AGING_31_60
    ,T.AGING_61_90
    ,T.AGING_91_180
    ,T.AGING_181_365
    ,T.AGING_366_730
    ,T.AGING_731_1095
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825
    ,T.AGING_1826
    ,(CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END) AGING_MINUS
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T13.SUM_ABC_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_C_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_0_30
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T14.SUM_ABC_31_60,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T4.SUM_C_31_60,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_31_60    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T15.SUM_ABC_61_90,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T5.SUM_C_61_90,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_61_90    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T16.SUM_ABC_91_180,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T6.SUM_C_91_180,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_91_180    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T17.SUM_ABC_181_365,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T7.SUM_C_181_365,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_181_365     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T18.SUM_ABC_366_730,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T8.SUM_C_366_730,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_366_730
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T19.SUM_ABC_731_1095,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T9.SUM_C_731_1095,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_731_1095
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T20.SUM_ABC_1096_1460,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T10.SUM_C_1096_1460,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_1096_1460
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T21.SUM_ABC_1461_1825,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T11.SUM_C_1461_1825,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_1461_1825
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T22.SUM_ABC_1826,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T12.SUM_C_1826,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_1826
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_AB  -- AB合计金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_EST  -- 暂估账龄金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' )
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_0_30  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）0-30日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_31_60  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）31-60日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_61_90  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）61-90日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_91_180  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）91-180日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 91 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 180
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_181_365  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）181-365日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T7
ON T.COMP_CODE = T7.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T7.SUPPLIER_CODE

    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_ABC_731_1095  -- 科目分类A和B和C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）2-3年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 731 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1095
GROUP BY

FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1096 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460

ON T.COMP_CODE = T20.COMP_CODE
