SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_ABC_1461_1825  -- 科目分类A和B和C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）4-5年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T21
ON T.COMP_CODE = T21.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T21.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_ABC_1826  -- 科目分类A和B和C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）5年以上金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826 
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T22
ON T.COMP_CODE = T22.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T22.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C   -- 科目C合计金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T25 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T25.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T25.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T13.SUM_ABC_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_C_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T14.SUM_ABC_31_60,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T4.SUM_C_31_60,0)
        ELSE 0
    END   
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T15.SUM_ABC_61_90,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T5.SUM_C_61_90,0)
        ELSE 0
    END     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T16.SUM_ABC_91_180,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T6.SUM_C_91_180,0)
        ELSE 0
    END    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T17.SUM_ABC_181_365,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T7.SUM_C_181_365,0)
        ELSE 0
    END     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T18.SUM_ABC_366_730,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T8.SUM_C_366_730,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T19.SUM_ABC_731_1095,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T9.SUM_C_731_1095,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T20.SUM_ABC_1096_1460,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T10.SUM_C_1096_1460,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T21.SUM_ABC_1461_1825,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T11.SUM_C_1461_1825,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T22.SUM_ABC_1826,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) > 0 THEN NVL(T12.SUM_C_1826,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
) T
) T
) T
;

/* 计算科目分类为应付的账龄，插入中间表10 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M10_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,'C' AS ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,( T.AGING_0_30 + T.AGING_31_60 + T.AGING_61_90 + T.AGING_91_180 + T.AGING_181_365 ) AS AGING_1_YEAR                                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0 
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS
        ELSE 0
    END AGING_1826                                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码    
    ,T.AGING_0_30
    ,T.AGING_31_60
    ,T.AGING_61_90
    ,T.AGING_91_180
    ,T.AGING_181_365
    ,T.AGING_366_730
    ,T.AGING_731_1095
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825
    ,T.AGING_1826
    ,(CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END) AGING_MINUS
--    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_AB < 0 THEN NVL(T23.SUM_C_MINUS,0) ELSE NVL(T24.SUM_ABC_MINUS,0) END AGING_MINUS
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T13.SUM_ABC_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0) )
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T3.SUM_C_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0) )
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_0_30
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T14.SUM_ABC_31_60,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1* NVL(T4.SUM_C_31_60,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_31_60    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T15.SUM_ABC_61_90,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T5.SUM_C_61_90,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_61_90    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T16.SUM_ABC_91_180,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T6.SUM_C_91_180,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_91_180    
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T17.SUM_ABC_181_365,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T7.SUM_C_181_365,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_181_365     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T18.SUM_ABC_366_730,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T8.SUM_C_366_730,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_366_730
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T19.SUM_ABC_731_1095,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T9.SUM_C_731_1095,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_731_1095
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T20.SUM_ABC_1096_1460,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T10.SUM_C_1096_1460,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_1096_1460
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T21.SUM_ABC_1461_1825,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T11.SUM_C_1461_1825,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_1461_1825
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T22.SUM_ABC_1826,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T12.SUM_C_1826,0)
        ELSE 0
    END AS AGING_1826
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_AB  -- AB合计金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_EST  -- 暂估账龄金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' )
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_0_30  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）0-30日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_31_60  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）31-60日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C_61_90  -- 科目分类C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）61-90日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE

WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 731 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1095

FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1096 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460

    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_ABC_1461_1825  -- 科目分类A和B和C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）4-5年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T21
ON T.COMP_CODE = T21.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T21.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_ABC_1826  -- 科目分类A和B和C（排除科目：0021210910和0021210920）5年以上金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('A','B','C') AND T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT NOT IN ( '0021210910','0021210920' ) AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826 
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T22
ON T.COMP_CODE = T22.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T22.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_C   -- 科目C合计金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('C')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T25 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T25.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T25.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T13.SUM_ABC_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0) )
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * ( NVL(T3.SUM_C_0_30,0) + NVL(T2.SUM_EST,0) )
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T14.SUM_ABC_31_60,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1* NVL(T4.SUM_C_31_60,0)
        ELSE 0
    END     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T15.SUM_ABC_61_90,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T5.SUM_C_61_90,0)
        ELSE 0
    END     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T16.SUM_ABC_91_180,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T6.SUM_C_91_180,0)
        ELSE 0
    END     
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T17.SUM_ABC_181_365,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T7.SUM_C_181_365,0)
        ELSE 0
    END      
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T18.SUM_ABC_366_730,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T8.SUM_C_366_730,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T19.SUM_ABC_731_1095,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T9.SUM_C_731_1095,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T20.SUM_ABC_1096_1460,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T10.SUM_C_1096_1460,0)
        ELSE 0
    END  
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T21.SUM_ABC_1461_1825,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T11.SUM_C_1461_1825,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) > 0 AND NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) + NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T22.SUM_ABC_1826,0)
          WHEN NVL(T1.SUM_AB,0) <= 0 AND NVL(T25.SUM_C,0) < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T12.SUM_C_1826,0)
        ELSE 0
    END 
) T
) T
) T
;

/* 计算科目分类为其他应付的账龄，插入中间表11 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M11_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,'D' AS ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,( T.AGING_0_30 + T.AGING_31_60 + T.AGING_61_90 + T.AGING_91_180 + T.AGING_181_365 ) AS AGING_1_YEAR                                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0 
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS
        ELSE 0
    END AGING_1826                                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码    
    ,T.AGING_0_30
    ,T.AGING_31_60
    ,T.AGING_61_90
    ,T.AGING_91_180
    ,T.AGING_181_365
    ,T.AGING_366_730
    ,T.AGING_731_1095
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825
    ,T.AGING_1826
    ,(CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END) AGING_MINUS
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T3.SUM_O_AP_0_30,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T13.SUM_O_AP1_0_30,0) END AGING_0_30
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T4.SUM_O_AP_31_60,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T14.SUM_O_AP1_31_60,0) END AGING_31_60
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T5.SUM_O_AP_61_90,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T15.SUM_O_AP1_61_90,0) END AGING_61_90
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T6.SUM_O_AP_91_180,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T16.SUM_O_AP1_91_180,0) END AGING_91_180
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T7.SUM_O_AP_181_365,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T17.SUM_O_AP1_181_365,0) END AGING_181_365
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T8.SUM_O_AP_366_730,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T18.SUM_O_AP1_366_730,0) END AGING_366_730
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T9.SUM_O_AP_731_1095,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T19.SUM_O_AP1_731_1095,0) END AGING_731_1095
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T10.SUM_O_AP_1096_1460,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T20.SUM_O_AP1_1096_1460,0) END AGING_1096_1460
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T11.SUM_O_AP_1461_1825,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T21.SUM_O_AP1_1461_1825,0) END AGING_1461_1825
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AP < 0 THEN -1 * NVL(T12.SUM_O_AP_1826,0) ELSE -1 * NVL(T22.SUM_O_AP1_1826,0) END AGING_1826
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP   -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100 合计金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100')
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP_0_30  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 0-30日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP_31_60  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 31-60日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP_61_90  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 61-90日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP_91_180  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 91-180日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 91 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 180
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP_181_365  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 181-365日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T7
ON T.COMP_CODE = T7.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T7.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP_366_730  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 1-2年金额合计

WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 366 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 730
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码

FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T

    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP1_91_180  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 91-180日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T

(
SELECT

    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AP1_181_365  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810140/0021810300/0021810200 181-365日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810140','0021810300','0021810200') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365

    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码

    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T17
ON T.COMP_CODE = T17.COMP_CODE

LEFT JOIN
