    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T10
ON T.COMP_CODE = T10.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T10.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR_1461_1825  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0011330140/0011330120 4-5年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T11
ON T.COMP_CODE = T11.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T11.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR_1826  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0021810117/0011330100/0011330140/0011330120 5年以上金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0021810117','0011330100','0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826 
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T12
ON T.COMP_CODE = T12.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T12.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_0_30  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 0-30日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T13
ON T.COMP_CODE = T13.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T13.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_31_60  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 31-60日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T14
ON T.COMP_CODE = T14.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T14.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_61_90  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 61-90日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T15
ON T.COMP_CODE = T15.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T15.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_91_180  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 91-180日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 91 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 180
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T16
ON T.COMP_CODE = T16.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T16.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_181_365  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 181-365日金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T17
ON T.COMP_CODE = T17.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T17.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_366_730  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 1-2年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 366 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 730
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T18
ON T.COMP_CODE = T18.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T18.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_731_1095  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 2-3年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 731 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1095
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T19
ON T.COMP_CODE = T19.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T19.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_1096_1460  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 3-4年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1096 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T20
ON T.COMP_CODE = T20.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T20.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_1461_1825  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 4-5年金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T21
ON T.COMP_CODE = T21.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T21.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS SUM_O_AR1_1826  -- 相同公司代码、供应商代码、总账科目为：0011330140/0011330120 5年以上金额合计
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT IN ('0011330140','0011330120') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826 
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    --,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
) T22
ON T.COMP_CODE = T22.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T22.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T3.SUM_O_AR_0_30,0) ELSE NVL(T13.SUM_O_AR1_0_30,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T4.SUM_O_AR_31_60,0) ELSE NVL(T14.SUM_O_AR1_31_60,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T5.SUM_O_AR_61_90,0) ELSE NVL(T15.SUM_O_AR1_61_90,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T6.SUM_O_AR_91_180,0) ELSE NVL(T16.SUM_O_AR1_91_180,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T7.SUM_O_AR_181_365,0) ELSE NVL(T17.SUM_O_AR1_181_365,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T8.SUM_O_AR_366_730,0) ELSE NVL(T18.SUM_O_AR1_366_730,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T9.SUM_O_AR_731_1095,0) ELSE NVL(T19.SUM_O_AR1_731_1095,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T10.SUM_O_AR_1096_1460,0) ELSE NVL(T20.SUM_O_AR1_1096_1460,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T11.SUM_O_AR_1461_1825,0) ELSE NVL(T21.SUM_O_AR1_1461_1825,0) END 
    ,CASE WHEN T1.SUM_O_AR > 0 THEN NVL(T12.SUM_O_AR_1826,0) ELSE NVL(T22.SUM_O_AR1_1826,0) END 
) T
) T
) T
;

/* 计算科目分类为应付票据银票的账龄，插入中间表13 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M13_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,'F' AS ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,( T.AGING_0_30 + T.AGING_31_60 + T.AGING_61_90 + T.AGING_91_180 + T.AGING_181_365 ) AS AGING_1_YEAR                                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0 
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS
        ELSE 0
    END AGING_1826                                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码    
    ,T.AGING_0_30
    ,T.AGING_31_60
    ,T.AGING_61_90
    ,T.AGING_91_180
    ,T.AGING_181_365
    ,T.AGING_366_730
    ,T.AGING_731_1095
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825
    ,T.AGING_1826
    ,(CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END) AGING_MINUS
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,NVL(T1.AGING_0_30,0)           AS AGING_0_30               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T2.AGING_31_60,0)          AS AGING_31_60              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T3.AGING_61_90,0)          AS AGING_61_90              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T4.AGING_91_180,0)         AS AGING_91_180             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T5.AGING_181_365,0)        AS AGING_181_365            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T6.AGING_366_730,0)        AS AGING_366_730            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T7.AGING_731_1095,0)       AS AGING_731_1095           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T8.AGING_1096_1460,0)      AS AGING_1096_1460          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T9.AGING_1461_1825,0)      AS AGING_1461_1825          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T10.AGING_1826,0)          AS AGING_1826               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_0_30         -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_31_60        -- 31-60日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_61_90        -- 61-90日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_91_180       -- 91-180日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 91 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 180
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_181_365      -- 181-365日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_366_730      -- 1-2年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 366 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 730
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_731_1095     -- 2-3年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 731 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1095
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T7
ON T.COMP_CODE = T7.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T7.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1096_1460    -- 3-4年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1096 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T8
ON T.COMP_CODE = T8.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T8.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1461_1825    -- 4-5年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T9
ON T.COMP_CODE = T9.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T9.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1826         -- 5年以上账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('F') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T10
ON T.COMP_CODE = T10.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T10.SUPPLIER_CODE

GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T1.AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T2.AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T3.AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T4.AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T5.AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T7.AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T8.AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T9.AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T10.AGING_1826                                             -- 5年以上账龄段金额
) T
) T
) T
;

/* 计算科目分类为应付票据商票的账龄，插入中间表14 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M14_D
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,'G' AS ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,( T.AGING_0_30 + T.AGING_31_60 + T.AGING_61_90 + T.AGING_91_180 + T.AGING_181_365 ) AS AGING_1_YEAR                                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0 
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS
        ELSE 0
    END AGING_1826                                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
   ,CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) >= 0
            THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) 
        ELSE 
            CASE WHEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END ) >= 0
                    THEN (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END ) + T.AGING_MINUS + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END ) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 < 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END )
                ELSE 0
            END 
    END AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM (
SELECT
    T.COMP_CODE                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码    
    ,T.AGING_0_30
    ,T.AGING_31_60
    ,T.AGING_61_90
    ,T.AGING_91_180
    ,T.AGING_181_365
    ,T.AGING_366_730
    ,T.AGING_731_1095
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825
    ,T.AGING_1826
    ,(CASE WHEN T.AGING_0_30 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_0_30 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_31_60 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_31_60 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_61_90 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_61_90 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_91_180 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_91_180 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_181_365 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_181_365 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_366_730 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_366_730 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_731_1095 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1096_1460 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1461_1825 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 END) + (CASE WHEN T.AGING_1826 >= 0 THEN 0 ELSE T.AGING_1826 END) AGING_MINUS
FROM (
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,NVL(T1.AGING_0_30,0)           AS AGING_0_30               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T2.AGING_31_60,0)          AS AGING_31_60              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T3.AGING_61_90,0)          AS AGING_61_90              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T4.AGING_91_180,0)         AS AGING_91_180             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T5.AGING_181_365,0)        AS AGING_181_365            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T6.AGING_366_730,0)        AS AGING_366_730            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T7.AGING_731_1095,0)       AS AGING_731_1095           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T8.AGING_1096_1460,0)      AS AGING_1096_1460          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T9.AGING_1461_1825,0)      AS AGING_1461_1825          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,NVL(T10.AGING_1826,0)          AS AGING_1826               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
LEFT JOIN 
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_0_30         -- 0-30日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 30
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T1 
ON T.COMP_CODE = T1.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T1.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_31_60        -- 31-60日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 31 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 60
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T2
ON T.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_61_90        -- 61-90日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 61 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 90
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T3
ON T.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_91_180       -- 91-180日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 91 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 180
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T4
ON T.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_181_365      -- 181-365日账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 181 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 365
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T5
ON T.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_366_730      -- 1-2年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 366 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 730
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T6
ON T.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_731_1095     -- 2-3年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 731 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1095
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T7
ON T.COMP_CODE = T7.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T7.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1096_1460    -- 3-4年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1096 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1460
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T8
ON T.COMP_CODE = T8.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T8.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1461_1825    -- 4-5年账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1461 AND T.DUE_DAYS <= 1825
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T9
ON T.COMP_CODE = T9.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T9.SUPPLIER_CODE

LEFT JOIN
(
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,-1 * SUM(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AMT) AS AGING_1826         -- 5年以上账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T
WHERE T.ACCOUNT_TYPE IN ('G') AND T.DUE_DAYS >= 1826
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
) T10
ON T.COMP_CODE = T10.COMP_CODE
AND T.SUPPLIER_CODE = T10.SUPPLIER_CODE
GROUP BY 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                             -- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T1.AGING_0_30                                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T2.AGING_31_60                                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T3.AGING_61_90                                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T4.AGING_91_180                                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T5.AGING_181_365                                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_366_730                                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T7.AGING_731_1095                                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T8.AGING_1096_1460                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T9.AGING_1461_1825                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T10.AGING_1826                                             -- 5年以上账龄段金额
) T
) T
) T
;

/* 将各科目分类数据插入中间表15 */
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M15_D
SELECT 
    T1.COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T1.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC             -- 总账科目描述
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                 -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                 -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_CODE                           -- 供应商代码
    ,T1.SUPPLIER_NAME                           -- 供应商全称
    ,T1.PAY_TERM_CODE                           -- 付款条款
    ,T1.PAY_TERM_DESC                           -- 付款条款描述
    ,T2.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING             -- 重分类前原币期末余额
    ,T3.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING              -- 重分类前本币期末余额
	,T4.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF          -- 重分类后原币期末余额
	,T5.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF           -- 重分类后本币期末余额
    ,T6.AGING_0_30                              -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_31_60                             -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_61_90                             -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_91_180                            -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_181_365                           -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_366_730                           -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_731_1095                          -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_1096_1460                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_1461_1825                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_1826                              -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T6.AGING_1_YEAR                            -- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM 
    DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M3_D T1
/* 重分类前原币期末余额 */
LEFT JOIN DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M4_D T2
ON T1.COMP_CODE = T2.COMP_CODE
AND T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T2.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T1.SUPPLIER_CODE = T2.SUPPLIER_CODE
/* 重分类前本币期末余额 */
LEFT JOIN DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M5_D T3
ON T1.COMP_CODE = T3.COMP_CODE
AND T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T3.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T1.SUPPLIER_CODE = T3.SUPPLIER_CODE
/* 重分类后原币期末余额 */
LEFT JOIN DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M6_D T4
ON T1.COMP_CODE = T4.COMP_CODE
AND T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T4.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T1.SUPPLIER_CODE = T4.SUPPLIER_CODE
/* 重分类后本币期末余额 */
LEFT JOIN DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M7_D T5
ON T1.COMP_CODE = T5.COMP_CODE
AND T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T5.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T1.SUPPLIER_CODE = T5.SUPPLIER_CODE
/* 重分类后本币各账龄段账龄 */
LEFT JOIN 
(
/* 科目分类为预提的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M8_D T

UNION ALL
/* 科目分类为预付的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M9_D T

UNION ALL
/* 科目分类为应付的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M10_D T

UNION ALL
/* 科目分类为其他应付的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M11_D T

UNION ALL
/* 科目分类为其他应收的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M12_D T

UNION ALL
/* 科目分类为应付票据银票的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M13_D T

UNION ALL
/* 科目分类为应付票据商票的账龄 */
SELECT 
    T.COMP_CODE                                                 -- 公司代码
    ,T.ACCOUNT_TYPE                                        		-- 科目分类
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.AGING_0_30                                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_31_60                                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_61_90                                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_91_180                                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_181_365                                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_366_730                                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_731_1095                                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1096_1460                                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1461_1825                                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1826                                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,T.AGING_1_YEAR                                            	-- 1年以内账龄段金额
FROM DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M14_D T
) T6
ON T1.COMP_CODE = T6.COMP_CODE
AND T1.ACCOUNT_TYPE = T6.ACCOUNT_TYPE
AND T1.SUPPLIER_CODE = T6.SUPPLIER_CODE

;
/*
将数据插入目标表 DWRFIN.DWR_AP_SUP_TRANS_RCSF_AGING_F
*/
INSERT OVERWRITE TABLE DWRFIN.DWR_AP_SUP_TRANS_RCSF_AGING_F
SELECT 
    '${PERIOD_ID_D}'                            AS QUERY_DATE                               -- 查询日期
    ,T.COMP_CODE                                AS COMP_CODE                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              AS GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  AS SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  AS SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                            AS SUPPLIER_CODE                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                            AS SUPPLIER_NAME                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                            AS PAY_TERM_CODE                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                            AS PAY_TERM_DESC                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,NVL(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING,0)       AS ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING              -- 重分类前原币期末余额
    ,NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING,0)        AS LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING               -- 重分类前本币期末余额
	,NVL(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0)    AS ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF           -- 重分类后原币期末余额
	,NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0)     AS LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF            -- 重分类后本币期末余额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
          WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) < 0 THEN T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF
            ELSE T.AGING_0_30
    END                                         AS AGING_0_30                               -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_31_60 
    END                                         AS AGING_31_60                              -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_61_90 
    END                                         AS AGING_61_90                              -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_91_180 
    END                                         AS AGING_91_180                             -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_181_365 
    END                                         AS AGING_181_365                            -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_366_730 
    END                                         AS AGING_366_730                            -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 
    END                                         AS AGING_731_1095                           -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 
    END                                         AS AGING_1096_1460                          -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 
    END                                         AS AGING_1461_1825                          -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1826 
    END                                         AS AGING_1826                               -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1_YEAR 
    END                                         AS AGING_1_YEAR                             -- 1年以内账龄段金额
    ,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()                        AS DW_LAST_UPDATE_DATE	                    -- 数据同步时间
FROM 
    DWRFIN.DWR_AP_M15_D T
GROUP BY
    T.COMP_CODE                                                -- 公司代码
    ,T.GENERAL_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_DESC                              -- 总账科目描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_CODE                                  -- 供应商属性代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_ATTRIBUTE_DESC                                  -- 供应商属性描述
    ,T.SUPPLIER_CODE                                            -- 供应商代码
    ,T.SUPPLIER_NAME                                            -- 供应商全称
    ,T.PAY_TERM_CODE                                            -- 付款条款
    ,T.PAY_TERM_DESC                                            -- 付款条款描述
    ,NVL(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING,0)                       -- 重分类前原币期末余额
    ,NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING,0)                        -- 重分类前本币期末余额
	,NVL(T.ORIGIN_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0)                    -- 重分类后原币期末余额
	,NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0)                     -- 重分类后本币期末余额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
          WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) < 0 THEN T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF
            ELSE T.AGING_0_30
    END                                                         -- 0-30日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_31_60 
    END                                                         -- 31-60日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_61_90 
    END                                                         -- 61-90日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_91_180 
    END                                                         -- 91-180日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_181_365 
    END                                                         -- 181-365日账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_366_730 
    END                                                         -- 1-2年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_731_1095 
    END                                                         -- 2-3年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1096_1460 
    END                                                         -- 3-4年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1461_1825 
    END                                                         -- 4-5年账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1826 
    END                                                         -- 5年以上账龄段金额
    ,CASE WHEN NVL(T.LOCAL_CU_END_BALANCE_AGING_AF,0) = 0 THEN 0 
            ELSE T.AGING_1_YEAR 
    END                                                         -- 1年以内账龄段金额
;
